root = {
  kind = "split"
  axis = "horizontal"
  ratio = 0.14
  first = {
    kind = "tabs"
    active = 0
    panels = ["place"]
  }
  second = {
    kind = "split"
    axis = "horizontal"
    ratio = 0.74
    first = {
      kind = "split"
      axis = "vertical"
      ratio = 0.62
      first = {
        kind = "tabs"
        active = 0
        panels = ["viewport"]
      }
      second = {
        kind = "tabs"
        active = 0
        panels = ["content", "console"]
      }
    }
    second = {
      kind = "split"
      axis = "vertical"
      ratio = 0.35
      first = {
        kind = "tabs"
        active = 0
        panels = ["outliner"]
      }
      second = {
        kind = "tabs"
        active = 0
        panels = ["inspector", "world"]
      }
    }
  }
}
